Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the devices framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 21. The resulting operator achieves bound O(852/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to devices structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 21/852 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a devices algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 627. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 132/4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the devices decomposition. By minimality, the element a^627 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 132-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
